      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 32, top: 20, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.5)', letterSpacing: '3px' }}>ATIVAÇÕES</div>
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', right: 32, top: 20, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.38)', letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>cada dia é um neurônio | cada commit, uma ativação</div>
      {months.map(function(mo) {
        return <div key={'m' + mo.w} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: cx(mo.w) - 4, top: 46, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.5)' }}>{mo.t}</div>;
      })}
      {[['Seg', 1], ['Qua', 3], ['Sex', 5]].map(function(r) {
        return <div key={r[0]} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 34, top: cy(r[1]) - 7, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.45)' }}>{r[0]}</div>;
      })}
      <svg width="860" height="262" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0 }}>
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="sweep" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="rgba(255,90,100,0)" />
            <stop offset="85%" stopColor="rgba(255,90,100,0.28)" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="rgba(255,200,205,0.9)" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        {cells.map(function(c) {
          return <circle key={'b' + c.i} cx={cx(c.w)} cy={cy(c.d)} r="1.6" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.13)" />;
        })}
        {links.map(function(L, n) {
          return (
            <line key={'l' + n} x1={cx(L.a.w)} y1={cy(L.a.d)} x2={cx(L.b.w)} y2={cy(L.b.d)} stroke={RED} strokeWidth="1" strokeOpacity="0.3" opacity="0">
              <animate attributeName="opacity" dur={T + 's'} repeatCount="indefinite" calcMode="discrete" values="0;1;0" keyTimes={kt(tcol(L.b.w))} />
            </line>
          );
        })}
        {cells.filter(function(c) { return c.l > 0; }).map(function(c) {
          return (
            <g key={'a' + c.i} opacity="0">
              <animate attributeName="opacity" dur={T + 's'} repeatCount="indefinite" calcMode="discrete" values="0;1;0" keyTimes={kt(tcol(c.w))} />
              <circle cx={cx(c.w)} cy={cy(c.d)} r={R[c.l] + 3} fill={RED} opacity="0.18" />
              <circle cx={cx(c.w)} cy={cy(c.d)} r={R[c.l]} fill={RED} fillOpacity={OP[c.l]} />
              {c.l === 4 ? <circle cx={cx(c.w)} cy={cy(c.d)} r="1.6" fill="#ffffff" /> : null}
            </g>
          );
        })}
        <g>
          <animateTransform attributeName="transform" type="translate" dur={T + 's'} repeatCount="indefinite" values={'0 0;0 0;' + gw.toFixed(1) + ' 0;' + gw.toFixed(1) + ' 0'} keyTimes={'0;0.03;0.58;1'} />
          <animate attributeName="opacity" dur={T + 's'} repeatCount="indefinite" values="0;1;1;0;0" keyTimes="0;0.03;0.56;0.62;1" />
          <rect x={X0 - 60} y={Y0 - 10} width="62" height={6 * PY + 20} fill="url(#sweep)" />
        </g>
      </svg>
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 32, bottom: 22, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'rgba(235,235,235,0.85)', fontWeight: 700 }}>{active + ' dias com commit'}</span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.35)' }}>|</span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'rgba(220,220,220,0.65)' }}>{'maior sequência: ' + best + (best === 1 ? ' dia' : ' dias')}</span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.35)' }}>|</span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'rgba(220,220,220,0.65)' }}>no último ano</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 690, top: 232, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.45)' }}>menos</div>
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 806, top: 232, display: 'flex', fontSize: 10, color: 'rgba(210,210,210,0.45)' }}>mais</div>
      <svg width="860" height="262" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0 }}>
        <circle cx="730" cy="239" r="1.6" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.25)" />
        <circle cx="743" cy="239" r="2.7" fill={RED} fillOpacity="0.55" />
        <circle cx="757" cy="239" r="3.4" fill={RED} fillOpacity="0.72" />
        <circle cx="773" cy="239" r="4.2" fill={RED} fillOpacity="0.88" />
        <circle cx="790" cy="239" r="5" fill={RED} />
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
})()
